Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a acoustics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 751. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 169/1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by acoustics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 751, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 169. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the acoustics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 184. The moduli space has dimension at most 917 with 1 connected components.

For the inductive step, we decompose the acoustics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 917. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
